```text
     1 | import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
     2 | import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
     3 | 
     4 | const prisma = new PrismaClient();
     5 | 
     6 | async function main() {
     7 |   const email = "e2e.user@bba.local";
     8 |   const password = "E2Epass!234";
     9 |   const hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);
    10 | 
    11 |   const user = await prisma.user.upsert({
    12 |     where: { email },
    13 |     update: { hashedPassword: hash, role: "CLIENT", name: "E2E User" },
    14 |     create: { email, hashedPassword: hash, role: "CLIENT", name: "E2E User" },
    15 |   });
    16 | 
    17 |   const client = await prisma.client.upsert({
    18 |     where: { userId: user.id },
    19 |     update: {
    20 |       businessName: "E2E Test Holdings",
    21 |       stage: "STRATEGY",
    22 |       readinessScore: 66,
    23 |       programTrack: "Tax Readiness",
    24 |     },
    25 |     create: {
    26 |       userId: user.id,
    27 |       businessName: "E2E Test Holdings",
    28 |       stage: "STRATEGY",
    29 |       readinessScore: 66,
    30 |       programTrack: "Tax Readiness",
    31 |     },
    32 |   });
    33 | 
    34 |   await prisma.checklistItem.deleteMany({ where: { clientId: client.id } });
    35 |   await prisma.task.deleteMany({ where: { clientId: client.id } });
    36 |   await prisma.document.deleteMany({ where: { clientId: client.id } });
    37 |   await prisma.notification.deleteMany({ where: { userId: user.id } });
    38 | 
    39 |   await prisma.checklistItem.createMany({
    40 |     data: [
    41 |       { clientId: client.id, label: "Bank Statements (3 months)", category: "Financial", status: "COMPLETE", required: true, autoDetected: true },
    42 |       { clientId: client.id, label: "Federal Tax Returns (2 years)", category: "Tax", status: "IN_PROGRESS", required: true, autoDetected: false },
    43 |       { clientId: client.id, label: "Government-Issued ID", category: "Identification", status: "PENDING", required: true, autoDetected: false },
    44 |     ],
    45 |   });
    46 | 
    47 |   await prisma.task.createMany({
    48 |     data: [
    49 |       { clientId: client.id, title: "Upload missing 1099", priority: "HIGH", status: "OPEN" },
    50 |       { clientId: client.id, title: "Confirm filer information", priority: "MEDIUM", status: "IN_PROGRESS" },
    51 |     ],
    52 |   });
    53 | 
    54 |   await prisma.notification.createMany({
    55 |     data: [
    56 |       { userId: user.id, title: "Upload reminder", message: "Please upload your 1099 form.", type: "WARNING", read: false },
    57 |       { userId: user.id, title: "Checklist progress", message: "Checklist updated to 66%.", type: "INFO", read: true },
    58 |     ],
    59 |   });
    60 | 
    61 |   console.log(JSON.stringify({
    62 |     email,
    63 |     password,
    64 |     userId: user.id,
    65 |     clientId: client.id,
    66 |   }));
    67 | }
    68 | 
    69 | main()
    70 |   .catch((error) => {
    71 |     console.error(error);
    72 |     process.exit(1);
    73 |   })
    74 |   .finally(async () => {
    75 |     await prisma.$disconnect();
    76 |   });
```

```text
     7 | async function createClient(formData: FormData) {
     8 |   "use server";
     9 | 
    10 |   const businessName = String(formData.get("businessName") ?? "").trim();
    11 |   const ownerName = String(formData.get("ownerName") ?? "").trim();
    12 |   const ownerEmail = String(formData.get("ownerEmail") ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
    13 |   const ownerPassword = String(formData.get("ownerPassword") ?? "");
    14 |   const programTrack = String(formData.get("programTrack") ?? "").trim();
    15 | 
    16 |   if (!businessName || !ownerEmail || !ownerPassword) {
    17 |     redirect(`${ROUTES.ADMIN_CLIENT_NEW}?error=${encodeURIComponent("Business name, owner email, and password are required.")}`);
    18 |   }
    19 | 
    20 |   if (ownerPassword.length < 8) {
    21 |     redirect(`${ROUTES.ADMIN_CLIENT_NEW}?error=${encodeURIComponent("Owner password must be at least 8 characters.")}`);
    22 |   }
    23 | 
    24 |   const existingUser = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    25 |     where: { email: ownerEmail },
    26 |     include: { client: { select: { id: true } } },
    27 |   });
    28 | 
    29 |   if (existingUser?.client) {
    30 |     redirect(`${ROUTES.ADMIN_CLIENT_NEW}?error=${encodeURIComponent("That owner already has a client profile.")}`);
    31 |   }
    32 | 
    33 |   const hashedPassword = await hash(ownerPassword, 10);
    34 | 
    35 |   const user = existingUser
    36 |     ? await prisma.user.update({
    37 |         where: { id: existingUser.id },
    38 |         data: {
    39 |           name: ownerName || existingUser.name,
    40 |           role: "CLIENT",
    41 |           hashedPassword,
    42 |         },
    43 |       })
    44 |     : await prisma.user.create({
    45 |         data: {
    46 |           email: ownerEmail,
    47 |           name: ownerName || businessName,
    48 |           role: "CLIENT",
    49 |           hashedPassword,
    50 |         },
    51 |       });
    52 | 
    53 |   const client = await prisma.client.create({
    54 |     data: {
    55 |       userId: user.id,
    56 |       businessName,
    57 |       programTrack: programTrack || null,
    58 |       stage: "LEAD",
    59 |       readinessScore: 0,
    60 |     },
    61 |   });
    62 | 
    63 |   redirect(`${ROUTES.ADMIN_CLIENTS}/${client.id}`);
    64 | }
```

```text
     1 | import Link from "next/link";
     2 | import { ClientCard } from "@/components/admin/ClientCard";
     3 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     4 | import { ROUTES } from "@/lib/routes";
     5 | 
     6 | const STAGES = ["ALL", "LEAD", "INTAKE", "STRATEGY", "FOUNDATION", "ACTIVE", "FUNDED", "CLOSED"];
     7 | 
     8 | export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
     9 | 
    10 | export default async function ClientsPage({
    11 |   searchParams,
    12 | }: {
    13 |   searchParams: Promise<{ stage?: string }>;
    14 | }) {
    15 |   const { stage } = await searchParams;
    16 |   const activeStage = stage ?? "ALL";
    17 | 
    18 |   const clients = await prisma.client.findMany({
    19 |     where: activeStage === "ALL" ? undefined : { stage: activeStage },
    20 |     include: {
    21 |       user: { select: { name: true } },
    22 |     },
    23 |     orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" },
    24 |   });
    25 | 
    26 |   const countsByStage = await prisma.client.groupBy({
    27 |     by: ["stage"],
    28 |     _count: { _all: true },
    29 |   });
    30 | 
    31 |   const totalCount = countsByStage.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row._count._all, 0);
    32 |   const stageCounts = Object.fromEntries(

    59 |       </div>

    67 |             className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-colors

    69 |                 ? "bg-brand-blue text-white"

    85 |

    89 |           <p>No clients found for this stage.</p>
    90 |         </div>
    91 |       )}
    92 |     </div>
    93 |   );
    94 | }
```

```text
     9 |   PENDING: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700",
    10 |   FAILED: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
    11 |   REJECTED: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",

    18 |   FORM_1099: "1099",
    19 |   K1: "K-1",
    20 |   FORMATION_DOC: "Formation Doc",
    21 |   INVOICE: "Invoice",
    22 |   CONTRACT: "Contract",
    23 |   ID_DOCUMENT: "ID Document",
    24 |   OTHER: "Other",
    25 | };
    26 | 
    27 | function formatFileSize(bytes: number): string {
    28 |   if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
    29 |   if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`;
    30 |   return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
    31 | }
    32 | 
    33 | export default async function AdminDocumentsPage({
    34 |   searchParams,
    35 | }: {
    36 |   searchParams: Promise<{ type?: string; status?: string; q?: string }>;
    37 | }) {
    38 |   const { type, status, q } = await searchParams;
    39 | 
    40 |   const documents = await prisma.document.findMany({
    41 |     where: {
    42 |       ...(type ? { documentType: type } : {}),

    49 |               { client: { businessName: { contains: q } } },
    50 |             ],
    51 |           }
    52 |         : {}),
    53 |     },
    54 |     include: {
    55 |       client: { select: { id: true, businessName: true } },
    56 |     },
    57 |     orderBy: { uploadedAt: "desc" },
    58 |     take: 200,
    59 |   });
    60 | 
    61 |   return (
    62 |     <div className="p-6">
    63 |       <div className="mb-6">
    64 |         <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-blue">Document Review</h1>

    68 |       {/* Filters */}
    69 |       <form className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-4 mb-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">

    71 |           <option value="">All Types</option>
    72 |           {Object.entries(typeLabels).map(([k, v]) => (
    73 |             <option key={k} value={k}>{v}</option>
    74 |           ))}
    75 |         </select>
    76 |         <select name="status" defaultValue={status ?? ""} className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue">
    77 |           <option value="">All Statuses</option>
    78 |           <option value="PENDING">Pending</option>
    79 |           <option value="PROCESSING">Processing</option>
    80 |           <option value="PROCESSED">Processed</option>
    81 |           <option value="FAILED">Failed</option>
    82 |           <option value="REJECTED">Rejected</option>
    83 |         </select>
    84 |         <input
    85 |           name="q"
    86 |           type="text"
    87 |           placeholder="Search by client or filename..."
    88 |           defaultValue={q ?? ""}
    89 |           className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm flex-1 min-w-48 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue"
    90 |         />
    91 |         <button type="submit" className="bg-brand-blue text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-brand-blue-light transition-colors">
    92 |           Filter
    93 |         </button>
    94 |         <Link href="/admin/documents" className="border border-gray-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">
    95 |           Clear
    96 |         </Link>
    97 |       </form>
    98 | 
    99 |       {/* Table */}
   100 |       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
   101 |         <table className="w-full">
   102 |           <thead>
   103 |             <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-100">
   104 |               <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase">Document</th>
   105 |               <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase">Client</th>
   106 |               <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase">Type</th>
   107 |               <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase">Status</th>

   109 |               <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase">Size</th>
   110 |               <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase">Actions</th>
   111 |             </tr>
   112 |           </thead>
   113 |           <tbody>
   114 |             {documents.map((doc, i) => (
   115 |               <tr key={doc.id} className={`border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors ${i % 2 === 0 ? "" : "bg-gray-50/30"}`}>
```

```text
     1 | import { ReactNode } from "react";
     2 | import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
     3 | import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
     4 | import { Sidebar } from "@/components/layout/Sidebar";
     5 | import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
     6 | import { ADMIN_ROLES } from "@/lib/authHelpers";
     7 | import { buildLoginHref } from "@/lib/authRouting";
     8 | import { ROUTES } from "@/lib/routes";
     9 | import { logAuthRouteFailure } from "@/lib/runtimeLogger";
    10 | 
    11 | export default async function AdminLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    12 |   const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
    13 | 
    14 |   if (!session) {
    15 |     logAuthRouteFailure("missing-session", { route: ROUTES.ADMIN });
    16 |     redirect(buildLoginHref(ROUTES.ADMIN));
    17 |   }
    18 | 
    19 |   if (!(ADMIN_ROLES as readonly string[]).includes(session.user.role)) {
    20 |     logAuthRouteFailure("insufficient-role", {
    21 |       route: ROUTES.ADMIN,
    22 |       role: session.user.role,
    23 |     });
    24 |     redirect(ROUTES.PORTAL);
    25 |   }
    26 | 
    27 |   return (
    28 |     <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-gray-50">
    29 |       <Sidebar />
    30 |       <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">{children}</div>
    31 |     </div>
    32 |   );
    33 | }
```

```text
     1 | import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
     2 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     3 | import { requireAdminSession } from "@/lib/authHelpers";
     4 | 
     5 | export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
     6 |   try {
     7 |     const { error } = await requireAdminSession();
     8 |     if (error) return error;
     9 | 
    10 |     const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    11 |     const stage = searchParams.get("stage");
    12 |     const search = searchParams.get("search");
    13 | 
    14 |     const clients = await prisma.client.findMany({
    15 |       where: {
    16 |         ...(stage && { stage }),
    17 |         ...(search && {
    18 |           businessName: { contains: search },
    19 |         }),
    20 |       },
    21 |       include: { user: { select: { name: true, email: true } } },
    22 |       orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" },
    23 |     });
    24 | 
    25 |     return NextResponse.json(clients);
    26 |   } catch (error) {
    27 |     console.error("[ADMIN_CLIENTS_GET]", error);
    28 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, { status: 500 });
    29 |   }
    30 | }
```

```text
     1 | import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
     2 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     3 | import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
     4 | import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
     5 | 
     6 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
     7 |   try {
     8 |     const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
     9 |     if (!session) {
    10 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    11 |     }
    12 | 
    13 |     const body = await req.json();
    14 |     const { clientId, content, isInternal } = body;
    15 | 
    16 |     if (!clientId || !content) {
    17 |       return NextResponse.json(
    18 |         { error: "clientId and content are required" },
    19 |         { status: 400 }
    20 |       );
    21 |     }
    22 | 
    23 |     const note = await prisma.note.create({
    24 |       data: {
    25 |         authorId: session.user.id,
    26 |         clientId,
    27 |         content,
    28 |         isInternal: isInternal ?? false,
    29 |       },
    30 |       include: { author: { select: { name: true } } },
    31 |     });
    32 | 
    33 |     // Audit log
    34 |     await prisma.auditLog.create({
    35 |       data: {
    36 |         userId: session.user.id,
    37 |         action: "NOTE_CREATED",
    38 |         entityType: "Note",
    39 |         entityId: note.id,
    40 |         metadata: JSON.stringify({ clientId, isInternal }),
    41 |       },
    42 |     });
    43 | 
    44 |     return NextResponse.json(note, { status: 201 });
    45 |   } catch (error) {
    46 |     console.error("[NOTES_POST]", error);
    47 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, { status: 500 });
    48 |   }
    49 | }
```

```text
     6 | 
     7 | function canAccessClient(session: { user: { id: string; role: string } }, ownerUserId: string) {
     8 |   if ((ADMIN_ROLES as readonly string[]).includes(session.user.role)) {
     9 |     return true;
    10 |   }
    11 |   return session.user.id === ownerUserId;

    13 | 
    14 | export async function GET(
    15 |   req: NextRequest,

    17 | ) {
    18 |   try {
    19 |     const { id } = await params;
    20 |     const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
    21 |     if (!session) {
    22 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

    24 |

    31 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Client not found" }, { status: 404 });
    32 |     }
    33 | 
    34 |     if (!canAccessClient(session, owner.userId)) {
    35 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
    36 |     }
    37 |

    39 |       where: { id },

    41 |         user: { select: { name: true, email: true, image: true } },
    42 |         documents: { orderBy: { uploadedAt: "desc" } },
    43 |         checklistItems: { orderBy: { category: "asc" } },
    44 |         tasks: { orderBy: { dueDate: "asc" } },

    50 |     });
    51 | 
    52 |     if (!client) {

    54 |     }
    55 | 
    56 |     return NextResponse.json(client);
```

```text
     1 | import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
     2 | import { readFile } from "fs/promises";
     3 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     4 | import { runIngestionPipeline } from "@/lib/ingestion/pipeline";
     5 | import { updateChecklistFromDocument } from "@/lib/ingestion/checklist-updater";
     6 | import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
     7 | import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
     8 | import { ADMIN_ROLES } from "@/lib/authHelpers";
     9 | 
    10 | export async function POST(
    11 |   req: NextRequest,
    12 |   { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
    13 | ) {
    14 |   const { id } = await params;
    15 |   try {
    16 |     const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
    17 |     if (!session) {
    18 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    19 |     }
    20 | 
    21 |     const document = await prisma.document.findUnique({
    22 |       where: { id },
    23 |       include: {
    24 |         client: { select: { userId: true } },
    25 |       },
    26 |     });
    27 | 
    28 |     if (!document) {
    29 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Document not found" }, { status: 404 });
    30 |     }
    31 | 
    32 |     const isAdmin = (ADMIN_ROLES as readonly string[]).includes(session.user.role);
    33 |     const isOwner = document.client.userId === session.user.id;
    34 |     if (!isAdmin && !isOwner) {
    35 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
    36 |     }
    37 | 
    38 |     // Mark as processing
    39 |     await prisma.document.update({
    40 |       where: { id },
    41 |       data: { status: "PROCESSING" },
    42 |     });
    43 | 
    44 |     // Load file buffer
    45 |     const buffer = await readFile(document.storageKey);
    46 | 
    47 |     // Run ingestion pipeline
    48 |     const result = await runIngestionPipeline({
    49 |       documentId: document.id,
    50 |       clientId: document.clientId,
    51 |       filename: document.originalName,
    52 |       mimeType: document.mimeType,
    53 |       buffer,
    54 |       documentType: document.documentType,
    55 |     });
    56 | 
    57 |     // Update document with extracted data
    58 |     await prisma.document.update({
    59 |       where: { id },
    60 |       data: {
    61 |         status: "PROCESSED",
    62 |         extractedData: JSON.stringify(result.extractedData),
    63 |         documentType: result.documentType,
    64 |         classificationConfidence: result.confidence,
    65 |         period: result.period ?? null,
    66 |         processedAt: new Date(),
    67 |       },
    68 |     });
    69 | 
    70 |     // Update checklist based on document type
    71 |     await updateChecklistFromDocument(document.clientId, result.documentType);
    72 | 
    73 |     return NextResponse.json({
    74 |       success: true,
    75 |       documentId: id,
    76 |       documentType: result.documentType,
    77 |       extractedData: result.extractedData,
    78 |     });
    79 |   } catch (error) {
    80 |     console.error("[DOCUMENT_PROCESS]", error);
    81 | 
    82 |     await prisma.document.update({
    83 |       where: { id },
    84 |       data: { status: "FAILED" },
    85 |     }).catch(() => {});
    86 | 
    87 |     return NextResponse.json(
    88 |       { error: "Processing failed" },
    89 |       { status: 500 }
    90 |     );
    91 |   }
    92 | }
```

```text
    22 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
    23 |   try {
    24 |     const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
    25 |     if (!session) {
    26 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

    36 |         { error: "file and clientId are required" },

    38 |       );
    39 |     }
    40 |

    47 |

    52 |       );

    55 |     // Validate clientId - must be a cuid-like alphanumeric string, no slashes or dots
    56 |     if (!clientId || !/^[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(clientId) || clientId.length > 30) {
    57 |       return NextResponse.json(
    58 |         { error: "Invalid clientId" },

    60 |       );
    61 |     }
    62 |

    65 |       select: { userId: true },

    67 |

    70 |     }
    71 |

    73 |     const isOwner = client.userId === session.user.id;
    74 |     if (!isAdmin && !isOwner) {
    75 |       return NextResponse.json({ error: "Forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
    76 |     }
    77 | 
    78 |     // Classify document type if not provided
    79 |     const classification = classify(file.name, file.type);

    85 |
```

```text
     1 | import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
     2 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     3 | import { intakeSubmissionSchema } from "@/lib/validations/intake";
     4 | 
     5 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
     6 |   try {
     7 |     const body = await req.json();
     8 |     const validated = intakeSubmissionSchema.safeParse(body);
     9 | 
    10 |     if (!validated.success) {
    11 |       return NextResponse.json(
    12 |         { error: "Invalid data", details: validated.error.flatten() },
    13 |         { status: 400 }
    14 |       );
    15 |     }
    16 | 
    17 |     const { clientId, answers, step, completed } = validated.data;
    18 | 
    19 |     // If clientId is "pending", create a guest lead client record
    20 |     let resolvedClientId = clientId;
    21 |     if (clientId === "pending") {
    22 |       const answerMap = answers as Record<string, unknown>;
    23 |       const businessName = (answerMap.businessName as string) ?? "New Applicant";
    24 |       const ownerEmail = (answerMap.ownerEmail as string) ?? `guest-${Date.now()}@pending.bba`;
    25 | 
    26 |       // Find or create a user for this email
    27 |       let user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: ownerEmail } });
    28 |       if (!user) {
    29 |         user = await prisma.user.create({
    30 |           data: { email: ownerEmail, name: answerMap.ownerName as string ?? businessName, role: "CLIENT" },
    31 |         });
    32 |       }
    33 | 
    34 |       // Find or create the client record
    35 |       const existing = await prisma.client.findUnique({ where: { userId: user.id } });
    36 |       if (existing) {
    37 |         resolvedClientId = existing.id;
    38 |       } else {
    39 |         const newClient = await prisma.client.create({
    40 |           data: { userId: user.id, businessName, stage: "LEAD" },
    41 |         });
    42 |         resolvedClientId = newClient.id;
    43 |       }
    44 |     }
    45 | 
    46 |     const submission = await prisma.intakeSubmission.create({
    47 |       data: {
    48 |         clientId: resolvedClientId,
    49 |         answers: JSON.stringify(answers),
    50 |         step,
    51 |         completed,
    52 |         submittedAt: completed ? new Date() : null,
    53 |       },
    54 |     });
    55 | 
    56 |     // If completed, advance client to INTAKE stage
    57 |     if (completed) {
    58 |       await prisma.client.update({
    59 |         where: { id: resolvedClientId },
    60 |         data: { stage: "INTAKE" },
    61 |       });
    62 |     }
    63 | 
    64 |     return NextResponse.json({ success: true, submission }, { status: 201 });
    65 |   } catch (error) {
    66 |     console.error("[INTAKE_POST]", error);
    67 |     return NextResponse.json(
    68 |       { error: "Internal server error" },
    69 |       { status: 500 }
    70 |     );
    71 |   }
    72 | }
```

```text
     1 | "use client";
```

```text
     3 | import { useState } from "react";
     4 | import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
     5 | import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/Textarea";
     6 | 
     7 | interface NoteItem {
     8 |   id: string;
     9 |   content: string;
    10 |   isInternal: boolean;
    11 |   createdAt: string;
    12 |   authorName: string;
    13 | }
    14 | 
    15 | export function ClientNotesPanel({
    16 |   clientId,
    17 |   initialNotes,
    18 | }: {
    19 |   clientId: string;
    20 |   initialNotes: NoteItem[];
    21 | }) {
    22 |   const [notes, setNotes] = useState<NoteItem[]>(initialNotes);
    23 |   const [content, setContent] = useState("");
    24 |   const [isInternal, setIsInternal] = useState(false);
    25 |   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    26 |   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    27 | 
    28 |   const submit = async () => {
    29 |     const trimmed = content.trim();
    30 |     if (!trimmed) {
    31 |       return;
    32 |     }
    33 | 
    34 |     setSaving(true);
    35 |     setError(null);
    36 | 
    37 |     try {
    38 |       const response = await fetch("/api/admin/notes", {
    39 |         method: "POST",
    40 |         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    41 |         body: JSON.stringify({
    42 |           clientId,
    43 |           content: trimmed,
    44 |           isInternal,
    45 |         }),
    46 |       });
    47 | 
    48 |       if (!response.ok) {
    49 |         const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({ error: "Failed to add note" }));
    50 |         setError(payload.error ?? "Failed to add note");
    51 |         return;
    52 |       }
    53 | 
    54 |       const note = await response.json();
    55 |       const mapped: NoteItem = {
    56 |         id: note.id,
    57 |         content: note.content,
    58 |         isInternal: note.isInternal,
    59 |         createdAt: new Date(note.createdAt).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    60 |         authorName: note.author?.name ?? "Unknown",
    61 |       };
    62 | 
    63 |       setNotes((prev) => [mapped, ...prev]);
    64 |       setContent("");
    65 |       setIsInternal(false);
    66 |     } finally {
    67 |       setSaving(false);
    68 |     }
    69 |   };
    70 | 
    71 |   return (
    72 |     <div>
    73 |       <div className="space-y-3">
    74 |         {notes.map((note) => (
    75 |           <div key={note.id} className={`p-4 rounded-lg ${note.isInternal ? "bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200" : "bg-gray-50"}`}>
    76 |             {note.isInternal && (
    77 |               <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-yellow-600 bg-yellow-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full mb-2 inline-block">
    78 |                 Internal
    79 |               </span>
    80 |             )}
    81 |             <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">{note.content}</p>
    82 |             <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">{note.authorName} · {note.createdAt}</p>
    83 |           </div>
    84 |         ))}
    85 | 
    86 |         {notes.length === 0 && (
    87 |           <div className="text-sm text-gray-400">No notes yet.</div>
    88 |         )}
    89 |       </div>
    90 | 
    91 |       <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white">
    92 |         <Textarea
    93 |           label="Add Note"
    94 |           placeholder="Write an update, client call summary, or follow-up detail..."
    95 |           value={content}
    96 |           onChange={(event) => setContent(event.target.value)}
    97 |           rows={3}
    98 |         />
    99 | 
   100 |         <label className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
   101 |           <input
   102 |             type="checkbox"
   103 |             className="rounded border-gray-300"
   104 |             checked={isInternal}
   105 |             onChange={(event) => setIsInternal(event.target.checked)}
   106 |           />
   107 |           Mark as internal note
   108 |         </label>
   109 | 
   110 |         {error && (
   111 |           <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-600">{error}</div>
   112 |         )}
   113 | 
   114 |         <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end">
   115 |           <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={submit} loading={saving}>
   116 |             Add Note
   117 |           </Button>
   118 |         </div>
   119 |       </div>
   120 |     </div>
   121 |   );
   122 | }
```

```text
    26 | export function TaskPanel({ clientId }: { clientId: string }) {
    27 |   const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);

    73 |         body: JSON.stringify({

    80 | 
    81 |       if (!response.ok) return;
    82 |       const task = await response.json();
    83 |       setTasks((prev) => [{

    87 |         priority: task.priority,
    88 |         status: task.status,
    89 |         dueDate: task.dueDate ? new Date(task.dueDate).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : undefined,

    92 |       setNewTask({ title: "", priority: "MEDIUM", dueDate: "" });

   112 |

   198 |         onClose={() => setShowAddDialog(false)}
   199 |         title="Add New Task"
   200 |         footer={
   201 |           <>
   202 |             <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setShowAddDialog(false)} disabled={saving}>Cancel</Button>
   203 |             <Button variant="secondary" onClick={addTask} loading={saving}>Add Task</Button>
   204 |           </>
   205 |         }
   206 |       >
   207 |         <div className="space-y-4">
   208 |           <Input
   209 |             label="Task Title"
   210 |             required
   211 |             placeholder="e.g., Request updated bank statements"
   212 |             value={newTask.title}
   213 |             onChange={(e) => setNewTask((prev) => ({ ...prev, title: e.target.value }))}
   214 |           />
   215 |           <Select
   216 |             label="Priority"
   217 |             options={["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH", "URGENT"]}
   218 |             value={newTask.priority}
   219 |             onChange={(e) => setNewTask((prev) => ({ ...prev, priority: e.target.value }))}
   220 |           />
   221 |           <Input
   222 |             label="Due Date"
   223 |             type="date"
   224 |             value={newTask.dueDate}
   225 |             onChange={(e) => setNewTask((prev) => ({ ...prev, dueDate: e.target.value }))}
   226 |           />
   227 |         </div>
   228 |       </Dialog>
   229 |     </div>
   230 |   );
   231 | }
```

```text
     1 | "use client";
     2 | 
     3 | import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
     4 | import { signIn } from "next-auth/react";
     5 | import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     6 | import { Input } from "@/components/ui/Input";
     7 | import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
     8 | 
     9 | export function LoginForm({ callbackUrl }: { callbackUrl: string }) {
    10 |   const router = useRouter();
    11 |   const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    12 |   const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
    13 |   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    14 |   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    15 | 
    16 |   const onSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    17 |     event.preventDefault();
    18 | 
    19 |     setLoading(true);
    20 |     setError(null);
    21 | 
    22 |     const response = await signIn("credentials", {
    23 |       email,
    24 |       password,
    25 |       callbackUrl,
    26 |       redirect: false,
    27 |     });
    28 | 
    29 |     setLoading(false);
    30 | 
    31 |     if (!response || response.error) {
    32 |       setError("Invalid email or password.");
    33 |       return;
    34 |     }
    35 | 
    36 |     router.push(response.url ?? callbackUrl);
    37 |     router.refresh();
    38 |   };
    39 | 
    40 |   return (
    41 |     <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 py-12 px-4">
    42 |       <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 p-8">
    43 |         <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-blue">Sign in</h1>
    44 |         <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">Access your client portal and workflow dashboard.</p>
    45 | 
    46 |         <form className="mt-6 space-y-4" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
    47 |           <Input
    48 |             label="Email"
    49 |             type="email"
    50 |             required
    51 |             value={email}
    52 |             onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
    53 |           />
    54 |           <Input
    55 |             label="Password"
    56 |             type="password"
    57 |             required
    58 |             value={password}
    59 |             onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
    60 |           />
    61 | 
    62 |           {error && (
    63 |             <div className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
    64 |               {error}
    65 |             </div>
    66 |           )}
    67 | 
    68 |           <Button type="submit" variant="secondary" loading={loading} className="w-full">
    69 |             Sign in
    70 |           </Button>
    71 |         </form>
    72 |       </div>
    73 |     </div>
    74 |   );
    75 | }
```

```text
   438 |               label="Prior Year Filing Status"
   439 |               required
   440 |               placeholder="How did last year's filing cycle go?"
   441 |               options={[
   442 |                 { value: "never", label: "First year with BBA" },
   443 |                 { value: "rejected", label: "Filed with unresolved notices" },
   444 |                 { value: "approved-insufficient", label: "Filed but missed optimization opportunities" },
   445 |                 { value: "other", label: "Other" },
   446 |               ]}
   447 |               value={(formData.previousFundingAttempts as string) ?? ""}
   448 |               onChange={(e) => update("previousFundingAttempts", e.target.value)}
   449 |               error={errors.previousFundingAttempts}
   450 |             />
   451 |           </div>
   452 |         )}
   453 | 
   454 |         {currentStep === 5 && (
   455 |           <div className="space-y-4">
   456 |             <div>
   457 |               <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
   458 |                 Which of these documents do you currently have available?
   459 |               </p>
   460 |               <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-4">
   461 |                 Do not worry if you do not have everything. We will guide you through what is needed.
   462 |               </p>
   463 |               <div className="space-y-2">
   464 |                 {AVAILABLE_DOCS.map((doc) => {
   465 |                   const selected = ((formData.availableDocuments as string[]) ?? []).includes(doc.value);
   466 |                   return (
   467 |                     <label
   468 |                       key={doc.value}
   469 |                       className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-4 border rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-all
   470 |                         ${selected ? "border-green-400 bg-green-50" : "border-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-50"}`}
   471 |                     >
   472 |                       <input
   473 |                         type="checkbox"
   474 |                         checked={selected}
   475 |                         onChange={() => toggleMultiSelect("availableDocuments", doc.value)}
   476 |                         className="text-green-500 rounded w-4 h-4"
   477 |                       />
   478 |                       <span className="text-sm">{doc.label}</span>
   479 |                     </label>
   480 |                   );
   481 |                 })}
   482 |               </div>
   483 |             </div>
   484 |             <Textarea
   485 |               label="Anything else you'd like us to know?"
   486 |               placeholder="Share any additional context about your return, life events, or tax goals..."
   487 |               value={(formData.additionalInfo as string) ?? ""}
   488 |               onChange={(e) => update("additionalInfo", e.target.value)}
   489 |               rows={3}
   490 |             />
   491 |             <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-blue-800">
   492 |               <strong>By submitting this form, you agree to:</strong>
   493 |               <ul className="list-disc list-inside mt-1 space-y-0.5">
   494 |                 <li>Allow BBA to process your information for tax preparation and advisory</li>
   495 |                 <li>Be contacted by a BBA preparer via secure portal, email, or phone</li>
   496 |                 <li>Our privacy policy and terms of service</li>
   497 |               </ul>
   498 |             </div>
   499 |           </div>
   500 |         )}
   501 | 
   502 |         {submitError && (
   503 |           <div className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
   504 |             {submitError}
   505 |           </div>
   506 |         )}
   507 | 
   508 |         {/* Navigation */}
   509 |         <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-gray-100">
   510 |           <Button
   511 |             variant="outline"
   512 |             onClick={handleBack}
   513 |             disabled={currentStep === 1}
   514 |           >
   515 |             ← Back
   516 |           </Button>
   517 |           <div className="text-sm text-gray-400">
   518 |             Step {currentStep} of {STEPS.length}
   519 |           </div>
   520 |           {currentStep < STEPS.length ? (
   521 |             <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleNext}>
   522 |               Continue →
   523 |             </Button>
   524 |           ) : (
   525 |             <Button
   526 |               variant="primary"
   527 |               onClick={handleSubmit}
   528 |               loading={loading}
   529 |             >
   530 |               Submit Tax Intake
   531 |             </Button>
   532 |           )}
   533 |         </div>
   534 |       </div>
   535 |     </div>
   536 |   );
   537 | }
```

```text
     1 | "use client";
     2 | 
     3 | import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
     4 | import { Progress } from "@/components/ui/Progress";
     5 | import { Badge, statusToBadgeVariant } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
     6 | 
     7 | interface ChecklistItemData {
     8 |   id: string;
     9 |   label: string;
    10 |   description?: string;
    11 |   category: string;
    12 |   status: "PENDING" | "IN_PROGRESS" | "COMPLETE" | "NOT_APPLICABLE";
    13 |   required: boolean;
    14 |   autoDetected: boolean;
    15 |   dueDate?: string;
    16 | }
    17 | 
    18 | export function ChecklistPanel({ clientId }: { clientId: string }) {
    19 |   const [items, setItems] = useState<ChecklistItemData[]>([]);
    20 |   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    21 | 
    22 |   useEffect(() => {
    23 |     const loadChecklist = async () => {
    24 |       setLoading(true);
    25 |       try {
    26 |         const response = await fetch(`/api/clients/${clientId}`);
    27 |         if (!response.ok) return;
    28 |         const data = await response.json();
    29 | 
    30 |         const mapped: ChecklistItemData[] = (data.checklistItems ?? []).map((item: {
    31 |           id: string;
    32 |           label: string;
    33 |           description?: string | null;
    34 |           category: string;
    35 |           status: "PENDING" | "IN_PROGRESS" | "COMPLETE" | "NOT_APPLICABLE";
    36 |           required: boolean;
    37 |           autoDetected: boolean;
    38 |           dueDate?: string | null;
    39 |         }) => ({
    40 |           id: item.id,
    41 |           label: item.label,
    42 |           description: item.description ?? undefined,
    43 |           category: item.category,
    44 |           status: item.status,
    45 |           required: item.required,
    46 |           autoDetected: item.autoDetected,
    47 |           dueDate: item.dueDate ? new Date(item.dueDate).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : undefined,
    48 |         }));
    49 | 
    50 |         setItems(mapped);
    51 |       } finally {
    52 |         setLoading(false);
    53 |       }
    54 |     };
    55 | 
    56 |     loadChecklist();
    57 |   }, [clientId]);
    58 | 
    59 |   const required = items.filter((i) => i.required);
    60 |   const completed = required.filter((i) => i.status === "COMPLETE");
    61 |   const completionPct = required.length > 0
    62 |     ? Math.round((completed.length / required.length) * 100)
    63 |     : 0;
    64 | 
    65 |   // Group by category
    66 |   const grouped = items.reduce<Record<string, ChecklistItemData[]>>((acc, item) => {
    67 |     if (!acc[item.category]) acc[item.category] = [];
    68 |     acc[item.category].push(item);
    69 |     return acc;
    70 |   }, {});
    71 | 
    72 |   const statusIcon = (status: string) => {
    73 |     switch (status) {
    74 |       case "COMPLETE": return "✅";
    75 |       case "IN_PROGRESS": return "🔄";
    76 |       case "NOT_APPLICABLE": return "➖";
    77 |       default: return "⏳";
    78 |     }
    79 |   };
    80 | 
    81 |   return (
    82 |     <div className="space-y-6">
    83 |       {loading && (
    84 |         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-sm text-gray-500">
    85 |           Loading checklist...
    86 |         </div>
    87 |       )}
    88 | 
    89 |       {/* Overall Progress */}
    90 |       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 p-6">
    91 |         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
    92 |           <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-brand-blue">Overall Progress</h2>
    93 |           <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-blue">{completionPct}%</span>
    94 |         </div>
    95 |         <Progress value={completionPct} color={completionPct >= 80 ? "green" : completionPct >= 50 ? "gold" : "blue"} size="lg" />
    96 |         <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">
    97 |           <span>{completed.length} of {required.length} required items complete</span>
    98 |           <span>{required.length - completed.length} remaining</span>
    99 |         </div>
   100 |       </div>
   101 | 
   102 |       {!loading && items.length === 0 && (
   103 |         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-200 p-8 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
   104 |           Your checklist is being prepared.
   105 |         </div>
   106 |       )}
   107 | 
   108 |       {/* Checklist Groups */}
   109 |       {Object.entries(grouped).map(([category, items]) => (
   110 |         <div key={category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
   111 |           <div className="bg-gray-50 px-6 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
   112 |             <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{category}</h3>
   113 |             <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
   114 |               {items.filter((i) => i.status === "COMPLETE").length}/{items.length} complete
   115 |             </span>
   116 |           </div>
   117 |           <div className="divide-y divide-gray-50">
   118 |             {items.map((item) => (
   119 |               <div key={item.id} className="flex items-start gap-4 px-6 py-4">
   120 |                 <span className="text-xl mt-0.5">{statusIcon(item.status)}</span>
   121 |                 <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
   122 |                   <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
   123 |                     <span className={`text-sm font-medium ${item.status === "NOT_APPLICABLE" ? "text-gray-400 line-through" : "text-gray-800"}`}>
   124 |                       {item.label}
   125 |                     </span>
   126 |                     {item.required && item.status !== "NOT_APPLICABLE" && (
   127 |                       <span className="text-xs text-red-500 font-medium">Required</span>
   128 |                     )}
   129 |                     {item.autoDetected && (
   130 |                       <span className="text-xs bg-sky-100 text-sky-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
   131 |                         Auto-detected
   132 |                       </span>
   133 |                     )}
   134 |                   </div>
   135 |                   {item.description && (
   136 |                     <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{item.description}</p>
   137 |                   )}
   138 |                   {item.dueDate && item.status !== "COMPLETE" && (
   139 |                     <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 mt-0.5 font-medium">Due: {item.dueDate}</p>
   140 |                   )}
   141 |                 </div>
   142 |                 <div className="shrink-0">
   143 |                   <Badge variant={statusToBadgeVariant(item.status)}>
   144 |                     {item.status.replace("_", " ")}
   145 |                   </Badge>
   146 |                 </div>
   147 |               </div>
   148 |             ))}
   149 |           </div>
   150 |         </div>
   151 |       ))}
   152 |     </div>
   153 |   );
   154 | }
```

```text
   120 |         setError(payload.error ?? "Upload failed");
   121 |         setFiles((prev) => prev.filter((f) => f.id !== tempId));
   122 |         continue;
   123 |       }
   124 | 
   125 |       const uploadPayload = await uploadResponse.json();
   126 |       const document = uploadPayload.document as {
   127 |         id: string;
   128 |         originalName: string;
   129 |         documentType: string;
   130 |         fileSize: number;
   131 |         status: "PENDING" | "PROCESSING" | "PROCESSED" | "FAILED";
   132 |         uploadedAt: string;
   133 |       };
   134 | 
   135 |       const persistedFile: UploadedFile = {
   136 |         id: document.id,
   137 |         name: file.name,
   138 |         type: document.documentType,
   139 |         size: formatSize(document.fileSize),
   140 |         status: "PROCESSING",
   141 |         uploadedAt: new Date(document.uploadedAt).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
   142 |       };
   143 | 
   144 |       setFiles((prev) =>
   145 |         prev.map((f) => (f.id === tempId ? persistedFile : f))
   146 |       );
   147 | 
   148 |       const processResponse = await fetch(`/api/documents/${document.id}/process`, {
   149 |         method: "POST",
   150 |       });
   151 | 
   152 |       setFiles((prev) =>
   153 |         prev.map((f) =>
   154 |           f.id === document.id
   155 |             ? { ...f, status: processResponse.ok ? "PROCESSED" : "FAILED" }
   156 |             : f
   157 |         )
   158 |       );
   159 | 
   160 |       if (!processResponse.ok) {
   161 |         setError("One or more documents failed processing. Please retry later.");
   162 |       }
   163 |     }
   164 | 
   165 |     setUploading(false);
   166 |     if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = "";
   167 |   };
   168 | 
   169 |   const formatSize = (bytes: number): string => {
   170 |     if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
   171 |     if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`;
   172 |     return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
   173 |   };
   174 | 
   175 |   return (
   176 |     <div className="space-y-6">
   177 |       {/* Upload Area */}
   178 |       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 p-6">
   179 |         <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-brand-blue mb-4">Upload Tax Documents</h2>
   180 | 
   181 |         {/* Type Selector */}
   182 |         <div className="mb-4">
   183 |           <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
   184 |             Document Type
   185 |           </label>
   186 |           <select
   187 |             value={selectedType}
   188 |             onChange={(e) => setSelectedType(e.target.value)}
   189 |             className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue"
   190 |           >
   191 |             {DOC_TYPES.map((t) => (
   192 |               <option key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</option>
   193 |             ))}
   194 |           </select>
   195 |         </div>
   196 | 
   197 |         {/* Drop Zone */}
   198 |         <div
   199 |           className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-8 text-center transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer
   200 |             ${dragging
   201 |               ? "border-brand-blue bg-blue-50 scale-[1.01]"
   202 |               : "border-gray-300 hover:border-brand-blue hover:bg-blue-50"}`}
   203 |           onDragOver={handleDragOver}
   204 |           onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
   205 |           onDrop={handleDrop}
   206 |           onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
   207 |         >
   208 |           <div className="text-4xl mb-3">📤</div>
   209 |           <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-1">
   210 |             Drag & drop files here
   211 |           </h3>
   212 |           <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">
   213 |             or click to browse your device
   214 |           </p>
   215 |           <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
   216 |             Supported: PDF, JPEG, PNG, XLSX, CSV - Max 25MB per file
   217 |           </p>
   218 |           <input
   219 |             ref={fileInputRef}
   220 |             type="file"
   221 |             multiple
   222 |             accept=".pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png,.xlsx,.csv,.tiff"
   223 |             onChange={handleFileSelect}
   224 |             className="hidden"
   225 |           />
   226 |         </div>
   227 | 
   228 |         {uploading && (
   229 |           <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-600 text-sm">
   230 |             <svg className="animate-spin w-4 h-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
```

```text
     1 | type BadgeVariant = "default" | "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info" | "gold";
     2 | 
     3 | interface BadgeProps {
     4 |   variant?: BadgeVariant;
     5 |   children: React.ReactNode;
     6 |   className?: string;
     7 | }
     8 | 
     9 | const variantClasses: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
    10 |   default: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700",
    11 |   success: "bg-green-100 text-green-800",
    12 |   warning: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800",
    13 |   error: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
    14 |   info: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800",
    15 |   gold: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-900",
    16 | };
    17 | 
    18 | export function Badge({ variant = "default", children, className = "" }: BadgeProps) {
    19 |   return (
    20 |     <span
    21 |       className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${variantClasses[variant]} ${className}`}
    22 |     >
    23 |       {children}
    24 |     </span>
    25 |   );
    26 | }
    27 | 
    28 | export function statusToBadgeVariant(status: string): BadgeVariant {
    29 |   switch (status) {
    30 |     case "PROCESSED":
    31 |     case "COMPLETE":
    32 |     case "FUNDED":
    33 |     case "DONE":
    34 |       return "success";
    35 |     case "PENDING":
    36 |     case "OPEN":
    37 |     case "LEAD":
    38 |       return "default";
    39 |     case "PROCESSING":
    40 |     case "IN_PROGRESS":
    41 |     case "STRATEGY":
    42 |     case "FOUNDATION":
    43 |       return "info";
    44 |     case "FAILED":
    45 |     case "REJECTED":
    46 |     case "CLOSED":
    47 |       return "error";
    48 |     case "WARNING":

    51 |     default:
```

```text
    20 |       },
    21 |       async authorize(credentials) {
    22 |         if (!credentials?.email || !credentials?.password) {
    23 |           return null;

    25 |

    45 |         };
```

```text
     1 | import { ROUTES } from "@/lib/routes";
     2 | 
     3 | const INTERNAL_PATH_PATTERN = /^\/(?!\/)/;
     4 | 
     5 | export function normalizeCallbackPath(
     6 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
     7 |   fallback: string = ROUTES.PORTAL
     8 | ): string {
     9 |   if (!raw) {
    10 |     return fallback;
    11 |   }
    12 | 
    13 |   let candidate = raw;
    14 |   try {
    15 |     candidate = decodeURIComponent(raw);
    16 |   } catch {
    17 |     return fallback;
    18 |   }
    19 | 
    20 |   candidate = candidate.trim();
    21 |   if (!INTERNAL_PATH_PATTERN.test(candidate)) {
    22 |     return fallback;
    23 |   }
    24 | 
    25 |   return candidate;
    26 | }
    27 | 
    28 | export function buildLoginHref(callbackPath: string): string {
    29 |   const safePath = normalizeCallbackPath(callbackPath, ROUTES.PORTAL);
    30 |   return `${ROUTES.LOGIN}?callbackUrl=${encodeURIComponent(safePath)}`;
    31 | }
```

```text
     1 | import { Transaction, ExtractedData } from "./extractor";
     2 | 
     3 | export interface BankAnalysisResult {
     4 |   period: string;
     5 |   totalDeposits: number;
     6 |   totalWithdrawals: number;
     7 |   avgMonthlyDeposits: number;
     8 |   avgMonthlyWithdrawals: number;
     9 |   netCashflow: number;
    10 |   overdraftCount: number;
    11 |   nsfCount: number;
    12 |   recurringTransactions: RecurringTransaction[];
    13 |   lenderReadySummary: LenderReadySummary;
    14 | }
    15 | 
    16 | export interface RecurringTransaction {
    17 |   description: string;
    18 |   amount: number;
    19 |   frequency: string;
    20 |   occurrences: number;
    21 |   type: "credit" | "debit";
    22 | }
    23 | 
    24 | export interface LenderReadySummary {
    25 |   period: string;
    26 |   totalMonths: number;
    27 |   averageMonthlyRevenue: number;
    28 |   averageMonthlyExpenses: number;
    29 |   netMonthlyIncome: number;
    30 |   overdraftRisk: "LOW" | "MEDIUM" | "HIGH";
    31 |   cashflowTrend: "POSITIVE" | "NEUTRAL" | "NEGATIVE";
    32 |   keyStrengths: string[];
    33 |   keyRisks: string[];
    34 | }
    35 | 
    36 | export function analyzeBankStatement(
    37 |   extractedData: ExtractedData,
    38 |   filename: string
    39 | ): BankAnalysisResult {
    40 |   const transactions = extractedData.transactions ?? [];
    41 |   const period = extractedData.period ?? extractPeriodFromFilename(filename) ?? "Unknown Period";
    42 | 
    43 |   const credits = transactions.filter((t) => t.type === "credit");
    44 |   const debits = transactions.filter((t) => t.type === "debit");
    45 | 
    46 |   const totalDeposits = credits.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount, 0);
    47 |   const totalWithdrawals = debits.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount, 0);
    48 |   const netCashflow = totalDeposits - totalWithdrawals;
    49 | 
    50 |   // Count overdraft/NSF indicators
    51 |   const overdraftCount = countOverdrafts(transactions);
    52 |   const nsfCount = countNSF(transactions);
    53 | 
    54 |   // Estimate months from period string
    55 |   const totalMonths = estimateMonthCount(period, transactions);
    56 |   const avgMonthlyDeposits = totalMonths > 0 ? totalDeposits / totalMonths : totalDeposits;
    57 |   const avgMonthlyWithdrawals = totalMonths > 0 ? totalWithdrawals / totalMonths : totalWithdrawals;
    58 | 
    59 |   const recurringTransactions = detectRecurring(transactions);
    60 |   const lenderReadySummary = buildLenderSummary({
    61 |     period,
    62 |     totalMonths,
    63 |     avgMonthlyDeposits,
    64 |     avgMonthlyWithdrawals,
    65 |     overdraftCount,
    66 |     nsfCount,
    67 |     netCashflow,
    68 |   });
    69 | 
    70 |   return {
    71 |     period,
    72 |     totalDeposits,
    73 |     totalWithdrawals,
    74 |     avgMonthlyDeposits,
    75 |     avgMonthlyWithdrawals,
    76 |     netCashflow,
    77 |     overdraftCount,
    78 |     nsfCount,
    79 |     recurringTransactions,
    80 |     lenderReadySummary,
    81 |   };
    82 | }
    83 | 
    84 | function countOverdrafts(transactions: Transaction[]): number {
    85 |   return transactions.filter(
    86 |     (t) =>
    87 |       /overdraft|od fee|overdrawn/i.test(t.description) ||
    88 |       (t.balance !== undefined && t.balance < 0)
    89 |   ).length;
    90 | }
    91 | 
    92 | function countNSF(transactions: Transaction[]): number {
    93 |   return transactions.filter((t) => /nsf|insufficient funds|returned/i.test(t.description)).length;
    94 | }
    95 | 
    96 | function estimateMonthCount(period: string, transactions: Transaction[]): number {
    97 |   // Try to parse from period string
    98 |   const rangeMatch = period.match(/(\w+\s+\d{4})\s*[-–]\s*(\w+\s+\d{4})/);
    99 |   if (rangeMatch) {
   100 |     // Rough estimate: count months between two dates
   101 |     return 3; // Default to 3 for typical bank statement periods
   102 |   }
   103 | 
   104 |   // Single month
   105 |   if (/\b(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)/i.test(period)) {
   106 |     return 1;
   107 |   }
   108 | 
   109 |   // Use transaction date range
   110 |   if (transactions.length > 0) {
   111 |     const dates = transactions
   112 |       .map((t) => t.date)
   113 |       .filter(Boolean)
   114 |       .sort();
   115 |     if (dates.length >= 2) {
   116 |       const diffDays = 30; // simplified
   117 |       return Math.max(1, Math.round(diffDays / 30));
   118 |     }
   119 |   }
   120 | 
   121 |   return 1;
   122 | }
   123 | 
   124 | function detectRecurring(transactions: Transaction[]): RecurringTransaction[] {
   125 |   const grouped: Record<string, Transaction[]> = {};
   126 | 
   127 |   for (const tx of transactions) {
   128 |     const key = normalizeDescription(tx.description) + "_" + tx.amount.toFixed(2);
   129 |     if (!grouped[key]) grouped[key] = [];
   130 |     grouped[key].push(tx);
   131 |   }
   132 | 
   133 |   const recurring: RecurringTransaction[] = [];
   134 | 
   135 |   for (const [, txs] of Object.entries(grouped)) {
   136 |     if (txs.length >= 2) {
   137 |       recurring.push({
   138 |         description: txs[0].description,
   139 |         amount: txs[0].amount,
   140 |         frequency: txs.length >= 3 ? "monthly" : "occasional",
   141 |         occurrences: txs.length,
   142 |         type: txs[0].type,
   143 |       });
   144 |     }
   145 |   }
   146 | 
   147 |   return recurring.sort((a, b) => b.occurrences - a.occurrences).slice(0, 20);
   148 | }
   149 | 
   150 | function normalizeDescription(desc: string): string {
   151 |   return desc
   152 |     .toLowerCase()
   153 |     .replace(/\d{4,}/g, "") // remove long numbers (ref numbers, dates)
   154 |     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   155 |     .trim()
   156 |     .slice(0, 30);
   157 | }
   158 | 
   159 | function buildLenderSummary(params: {
   160 |   period: string;
   161 |   totalMonths: number;
   162 |   avgMonthlyDeposits: number;
   163 |   avgMonthlyWithdrawals: number;
   164 |   overdraftCount: number;
   165 |   nsfCount: number;
   166 |   netCashflow: number;
   167 | }): LenderReadySummary {
   168 |   const { period, totalMonths, avgMonthlyDeposits, avgMonthlyWithdrawals, overdraftCount, nsfCount, netCashflow } = params;
   169 | 
   170 |   const totalIssues = overdraftCount + nsfCount;
   171 |   const overdraftRisk: LenderReadySummary["overdraftRisk"] =
   172 |     totalIssues === 0 ? "LOW" :
   173 |     totalIssues <= 2 ? "MEDIUM" : "HIGH";
   174 | 
   175 |   const cashflowTrend: LenderReadySummary["cashflowTrend"] =
   176 |     netCashflow > 0 ? "POSITIVE" :
   177 |     netCashflow === 0 ? "NEUTRAL" : "NEGATIVE";
   178 | 
   179 |   const keyStrengths: string[] = [];
   180 |   const keyRisks: string[] = [];
   181 | 
   182 |   if (avgMonthlyDeposits > 10000) {
   183 |     keyStrengths.push(`Strong monthly revenue averaging $${avgMonthlyDeposits.toLocaleString("en-US", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}`);
   184 |   }
   185 |   if (overdraftCount === 0) {
   186 |     keyStrengths.push("No overdraft incidents – demonstrates financial discipline");
   187 |   }
   188 |   if (netCashflow > 0) {
   189 |     keyStrengths.push("Positive net cashflow demonstrates ability to service debt");
   190 |   }
   191 | 
   192 |   if (overdraftCount > 0) {
   193 |     keyRisks.push(`${overdraftCount} overdraft incident(s) detected`);
   194 |   }
   195 |   if (nsfCount > 0) {
   196 |     keyRisks.push(`${nsfCount} NSF/returned payment(s) detected`);
   197 |   }
   198 |   if (avgMonthlyDeposits < 5000) {
   199 |     keyRisks.push("Monthly deposit volume may reduce tax-planning confidence");
   200 |   }
   201 | 
   202 |   return {
   203 |     period,
   204 |     totalMonths,
   205 |     averageMonthlyRevenue: avgMonthlyDeposits,
   206 |     averageMonthlyExpenses: avgMonthlyWithdrawals,
   207 |     netMonthlyIncome: avgMonthlyDeposits - avgMonthlyWithdrawals,
   208 |     overdraftRisk,
   209 |     cashflowTrend,
   210 |     keyStrengths,
   211 |     keyRisks,
   212 |   };
   213 | }
   214 | 
   215 | function extractPeriodFromFilename(filename: string): string | undefined {
   216 |   const match = filename.match(/(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)_?(\d{4})/i);
   217 |   if (match) return `${match[1]} ${match[2]}`;
   218 |   return undefined;
   219 | }
```

```text
     4 | // Maps document type to the checklist item labels it satisfies
     5 | const DOCUMENT_TO_CHECKLIST: Partial<Record<DocumentType, string[]>> = {
     6 |   BANK_STATEMENT: ["Bank Statements (3 months)", "Bank Statements"],
     7 |   TAX_RETURN: ["Business Tax Returns", "Business Tax Returns (2 years)", "Personal Tax Returns"],
     8 |   FORMATION_DOC: ["Business Formation Documents", "Formation Documents", "Articles of Incorporation", "Operating Agreement", "EIN Letter"],
     9 |   W2: ["W-2 Forms", "W2 Documents"],
    10 |   FORM_1099: ["1099 Forms", "1099 Documents"],
    11 |   K1: ["K-1 Schedules", "Schedule K-1"],
    12 |   ID_DOCUMENT: ["Owner ID / Driver's License", "Government-Issued ID", "Identification Documents"],
    13 |   CONTRACT: ["Business Contracts", "Client Contracts"],
    14 |   INVOICE: ["Business Invoices"],
    15 | };
    16 | 
    17 | export async function updateChecklistFromDocument(
    18 |   clientId: string,
    19 |   documentType: DocumentType
    20 | ): Promise<void> {
    21 |   const checklistLabels = DOCUMENT_TO_CHECKLIST[documentType];
    22 |   if (!checklistLabels || checklistLabels.length === 0) return;
    23 | 
    24 |   // Find matching checklist items for this client
    25 |   const items = await prisma.checklistItem.findMany({
    26 |     where: {
    27 |       clientId,
    28 |       status: { not: "COMPLETE" },
    29 |     },
    30 |   });
    31 | 
    32 |   const toUpdate = items.filter((item: { id: string; label: string }) =>
    33 |     checklistLabels.some(
    34 |       (label) =>
    35 |         item.label.toLowerCase().includes(label.toLowerCase()) ||
    36 |         label.toLowerCase().includes(item.label.toLowerCase())
    37 |     )
    38 |   );
    39 | 
    40 |   for (const item of toUpdate) {
    41 |     await prisma.checklistItem.update({
    42 |       where: { id: item.id },
    43 |       data: {
    44 |         status: "IN_PROGRESS",
    45 |         autoDetected: true,
    46 |       },
    47 |     });
    48 |   }
    49 | 
    50 |   // If we have 3+ bank statements processed, mark as complete
    51 |   if (documentType === "BANK_STATEMENT") {
    52 |     const bankStatementCount = await prisma.document.count({
    53 |       where: {
    54 |         clientId,
    55 |         documentType: "BANK_STATEMENT",
    56 |         status: "PROCESSED",
    57 |       },
    58 |     });
    59 | 
    60 |     if (bankStatementCount >= 3) {
    61 |       const bankItems = items.filter((item: { id: string; label: string }) =>
    62 |         item.label.toLowerCase().includes("bank statement")
    63 |       );
    64 |       for (const item of bankItems) {
    65 |         await prisma.checklistItem.update({
    66 |           where: { id: item.id },
    67 |           data: {
    68 |             status: "COMPLETE",
    69 |             completedAt: new Date(),
    70 |             autoDetected: true,
    71 |           },
    72 |         });
    73 |       }
    74 |     }
    75 |   }
    76 | 
    77 |   // Mark formation docs as complete on first upload
    78 |   if (documentType === "FORMATION_DOC") {
    79 |     for (const item of toUpdate) {
    80 |       await prisma.checklistItem.update({
    81 |         where: { id: item.id },
    82 |         data: {
    83 |           status: "COMPLETE",
    84 |           completedAt: new Date(),
    85 |           autoDetected: true,
    86 |         },
    87 |       });
    88 |     }
    89 |   }
    90 | 
    91 |   // Update client readiness score
    92 |   await recalculateReadinessScore(clientId);
    93 | }
    94 | 
    95 | async function recalculateReadinessScore(clientId: string): Promise<void> {
    96 |   const allItems = await prisma.checklistItem.findMany({
    97 |     where: { clientId },
    98 |   });
    99 | 
   100 |   if (allItems.length === 0) return;
   101 | 
   102 |   const required = allItems.filter((i: { required: boolean; status: string }) => i.required);
   103 |   const completed = required.filter((i: { required: boolean; status: string }) => i.status === "COMPLETE");
   104 | 
   105 |   const score = required.length > 0
   106 |     ? Math.round((completed.length / required.length) * 100)
   107 |     : 0;
   108 | 
   109 |   await prisma.client.update({
   110 |     where: { id: clientId },
   111 |     data: { readinessScore: score },
   112 |   });
   113 | }
```

```text
     1 | export type DocumentType =
     2 |   | "BANK_STATEMENT"
     3 |   | "TAX_RETURN"
     4 |   | "W2"
     5 |   | "FORM_1099"
     6 |   | "K1"
     7 |   | "FORMATION_DOC"
     8 |   | "INVOICE"
     9 |   | "CONTRACT"
    10 |   | "ID_DOCUMENT"
    11 |   | "OTHER";
    12 | 
    13 | export interface ClassificationResult {
    14 |   documentType: DocumentType;
    15 |   confidence: number;
    16 | }
    17 | 
    18 | // Rules-based classifier using filename and mime type patterns
    19 | const rules: Array<{
    20 |   patterns: RegExp[];
    21 |   documentType: DocumentType;
    22 |   confidence: number;
    23 | }> = [
    24 |   {
    25 |     patterns: [/bank.?statement/i, /account.?statement/i, /checking/i, /savings.?account/i, /stmt/i],
    26 |     documentType: "BANK_STATEMENT",
    27 |     confidence: 0.92,
    28 |   },
    29 |   {
    30 |     patterns: [/1040/i, /tax.?return/i, /schedule.?c/i, /form.?1040/i, /federal.?return/i],
    31 |     documentType: "TAX_RETURN",
    32 |     confidence: 0.93,
    33 |   },
    34 |   {
    35 |     patterns: [/w[-_]?2\b/i, /wage.?and.?tax/i, /\bw2\b/i],
    36 |     documentType: "W2",
    37 |     confidence: 0.95,
    38 |   },
    39 |   {
    40 |     patterns: [/1099/i],
    41 |     documentType: "FORM_1099",
    42 |     confidence: 0.95,
    43 |   },
    44 |   {
    45 |     patterns: [/k[-_]?1/i, /schedule.?k/i, /partner.*share/i],
    46 |     documentType: "K1",
    47 |     confidence: 0.92,
    48 |   },
    49 |   {
    50 |     patterns: [
    51 |       /articles.?of.?incorporation/i,
    52 |       /operating.?agreement/i,
    53 |       /ein.?letter/i,
    54 |       /formation/i,
    55 |       /certificate.?of.?organization/i,
    56 |       /bylaws/i,
    57 |     ],
    58 |     documentType: "FORMATION_DOC",
    59 |     confidence: 0.88,
    60 |   },
    61 |   {
    62 |     patterns: [/invoice/i, /purchase.?order/i, /billing/i],
    63 |     documentType: "INVOICE",
    64 |     confidence: 0.85,
    65 |   },
    66 |   {
    67 |     patterns: [/contract/i, /agreement/i, /service.?agreement/i],
    68 |     documentType: "CONTRACT",
    69 |     confidence: 0.82,
    70 |   },
    71 |   {
    72 |     patterns: [/passport/i, /driver.?licen/i, /id.?card/i, /identification/i, /dl[-_ ]/i],
    73 |     documentType: "ID_DOCUMENT",
    74 |     confidence: 0.88,
    75 |   },
    76 | ];
    77 | 
    78 | export function classify(filename: string, mimeType?: string): ClassificationResult {
    79 |   const normalized = filename.toLowerCase().replace(/[_]/g, " ");
    80 |   // Also test original (lowercased) for hyphenated patterns
    81 |   const original = filename.toLowerCase();
    82 | 
    83 |   for (const rule of rules) {
    84 |     for (const pattern of rule.patterns) {
    85 |       if (pattern.test(normalized) || pattern.test(original)) {
    86 |         return { documentType: rule.documentType, confidence: rule.confidence };
    87 |       }
    88 |     }
    89 |   }
    90 | 
    91 |   // Fallback: try from mime type context
    92 |   if (mimeType?.includes("image")) {
    93 |     return { documentType: "ID_DOCUMENT", confidence: 0.4 };
    94 |   }
    95 | 
    96 |   return { documentType: "OTHER", confidence: 0.5 };
    97 | }
    98 | 
    99 | export function classifyFromContent(
   100 |   filename: string,
   101 |   textContent: string,
   102 |   mimeType?: string
   103 | ): ClassificationResult {
   104 |   // First try filename
   105 |   const fromFilename = classify(filename, mimeType);
   106 |   if (fromFilename.confidence >= 0.85) return fromFilename;
   107 | 
   108 |   // Try content keywords
   109 |   const contentLower = textContent.toLowerCase();
   110 | 
   111 |   if (/bank statement|account statement|beginning balance|ending balance/i.test(contentLower)) {
   112 |     return { documentType: "BANK_STATEMENT", confidence: 0.9 };
   113 |   }
   114 |   if (/form 1040|u\.s\. individual income tax|adjusted gross income/i.test(contentLower)) {
   115 |     return { documentType: "TAX_RETURN", confidence: 0.9 };
   116 |   }
   117 |   if (/wages, tips, other compensation|employer's ein|employee's ssn/i.test(contentLower)) {
   118 |     return { documentType: "W2", confidence: 0.92 };
   119 |   }
   120 |   if (/nonemployee compensation|payer's tin|recipient's tin/i.test(contentLower)) {
   121 |     return { documentType: "FORM_1099", confidence: 0.92 };
   122 |   }
   123 |   if (/partner's share|schedule k-1|partnership/i.test(contentLower)) {
   124 |     return { documentType: "K1", confidence: 0.9 };
   125 |   }
   126 |   if (/articles of incorporation|operating agreement|registered agent/i.test(contentLower)) {
   127 |     return { documentType: "FORMATION_DOC", confidence: 0.88 };
   128 |   }
   129 | 
   130 |   return fromFilename;
   131 | }
```
