     1 | import { DocumentType } from "./classifier";
     2 | 
     3 | export interface ExtractedData {
     4 |   documentType: DocumentType;
     5 |   period?: string;
     6 |   // Bank statement fields
     7 |   accountNumber?: string;
     8 |   bankName?: string;
     9 |   accountHolder?: string;
    10 |   beginningBalance?: number;
    11 |   endingBalance?: number;
    12 |   transactions?: Transaction[];
    13 |   // Tax return fields
    14 |   taxYear?: string;
    15 |   totalIncome?: number;
    16 |   grossRevenue?: number;
    17 |   netProfit?: number;
    18 |   taxableIncome?: number;
    19 |   // W2 / 1099 fields
    20 |   employerName?: string;
    21 |   wages?: number;
    22 |   federalTaxWithheld?: number;
    23 |   nonemployeeCompensation?: number;
    24 |   // Formation doc fields
    25 |   entityName?: string;
    26 |   entityType?: string;
    27 |   stateOfFormation?: string;
    28 |   formationDate?: string;
    29 |   ein?: string;
    30 |   // Generic
    31 |   raw?: Record<string, unknown>;
    32 | }
    33 | 
    34 | export interface Transaction {
    35 |   date: string;
    36 |   description: string;
    37 |   amount: number;
    38 |   balance?: number;
    39 |   type: "credit" | "debit";
    40 | }
    41 | 
    42 | export function extract(
    43 |   documentType: DocumentType,
    44 |   filename: string,
    45 |   textContent: string
    46 | ): ExtractedData {
    47 |   const base: ExtractedData = { documentType };
    48 | 
    49 |   switch (documentType) {
    50 |     case "BANK_STATEMENT":
    51 |       return extractBankStatement(filename, textContent, base);
    52 |     case "TAX_RETURN":
    53 |       return extractTaxReturn(textContent, base);
    54 |     case "W2":
    55 |       return extractW2(textContent, base);
    56 |     case "FORM_1099":
    57 |       return extract1099(textContent, base);
    58 |     case "FORMATION_DOC":
    59 |       return extractFormationDoc(textContent, base);
    60 |     default:
    61 |       return { ...base, raw: { filename, contentLength: textContent.length } };
    62 |   }
    63 | }
    64 | 
    65 | function extractBankStatement(
    66 |   filename: string,
    67 |   text: string,
    68 |   base: ExtractedData
    69 | ): ExtractedData {
    70 |   // Extract period from filename or content
    71 |   const periodFromFilename = extractPeriodFromFilename(filename);
    72 |   const periodFromContent = extractPeriodFromText(text);
    73 |   const period = periodFromFilename ?? periodFromContent;
    74 | 
    75 |   // Extract bank name from common patterns
    76 |   const bankPatterns = [
    77 |     /chase bank/i,
    78 |     /bank of america/i,
    79 |     /wells fargo/i,
    80 |     /citibank/i,
    81 |     /us bank/i,
    82 |     /pnc bank/i,
    83 |     /td bank/i,
    84 |     /capital one/i,
    85 |   ];
    86 |   let bankName: string | undefined;
    87 |   for (const pat of bankPatterns) {
    88 |     const m = text.match(pat);
    89 |     if (m) {
    90 |       bankName = m[0];
    91 |       break;
    92 |     }
    93 |   }
    94 | 
    95 |   // Parse transactions from common CSV/text formats
    96 |   const transactions = parseTransactions(text);
    97 | 
    98 |   // Extract balances
    99 |   const beginBalMatch = text.match(/beginning balance[:\s]+\$?([\d,]+\.?\d*)/i);
   100 |   const endBalMatch = text.match(/ending balance[:\s]+\$?([\d,]+\.?\d*)/i);
   101 | 
   102 |   return {
   103 |     ...base,
   104 |     period,
   105 |     bankName,
   106 |     beginningBalance: beginBalMatch ? parseAmount(beginBalMatch[1]) : undefined,
   107 |     endingBalance: endBalMatch ? parseAmount(endBalMatch[1]) : undefined,
   108 |     transactions,
   109 |   };
   110 | }
   111 | 
   112 | function extractTaxReturn(text: string, base: ExtractedData): ExtractedData {
   113 |   const yearMatch = text.match(/tax year[:\s]+(\d{4})/i) ?? text.match(/\b(20\d{2})\b/);
   114 |   const incomeMatch = text.match(/total income[:\s]+\$?([\d,]+)/i);
   115 |   const netProfitMatch = text.match(/net profit[:\s]+\$?([\d,]+)/i) ??
   116 |     text.match(/net income[:\s]+\$?([\d,]+)/i);
   117 | 
   118 |   return {
   119 |     ...base,
   120 |     taxYear: yearMatch ? yearMatch[1] : undefined,
   121 |     totalIncome: incomeMatch ? parseAmount(incomeMatch[1]) : undefined,
   122 |     netProfit: netProfitMatch ? parseAmount(netProfitMatch[1]) : undefined,
   123 |   };
   124 | }
   125 | 
   126 | function extractW2(text: string, base: ExtractedData): ExtractedData {
   127 |   const wagesMatch = text.match(/wages[,\s]+tips[,\s]+other compensation\s+([\d,]+)/i);
   128 |   const fedTaxMatch = text.match(/federal income tax withheld[:\s]+([\d,]+)/i);
   129 |   const employerMatch = text.match(/employer'?s?\s+name[:\s]+([^\n]+)/i);
   130 | 
   131 |   return {
   132 |     ...base,
   133 |     wages: wagesMatch ? parseAmount(wagesMatch[1]) : undefined,
   134 |     federalTaxWithheld: fedTaxMatch ? parseAmount(fedTaxMatch[1]) : undefined,
   135 |     employerName: employerMatch ? employerMatch[1].trim() : undefined,
   136 |   };
   137 | }
   138 | 
   139 | function extract1099(text: string, base: ExtractedData): ExtractedData {
   140 |   const compMatch = text.match(/nonemployee compensation[:\s]+\$?([\d,]+)/i);
   141 | 
   142 |   return {
   143 |     ...base,
   144 |     nonemployeeCompensation: compMatch ? parseAmount(compMatch[1]) : undefined,
   145 |   };
   146 | }
   147 | 
   148 | function extractFormationDoc(text: string, base: ExtractedData): ExtractedData {
   149 |   const entityMatch = text.match(/(?:name of (?:company|llc|corporation)|entity name)[:\s]+([^\n]+)/i);
   150 |   const stateMatch = text.match(/state of (?:organization|incorporation|formation)[:\s]+([^\n]+)/i);
   151 |   const einMatch = text.match(/(?:ein|employer identification number)[:\s]+(\d{2}-\d{7})/i);
   152 |   const dateMatch = text.match(/(?:date of (?:organization|formation|incorporation))[:\s]+([^\n]+)/i);
   153 | 
   154 |   return {
   155 |     ...base,
   156 |     entityName: entityMatch ? entityMatch[1].trim() : undefined,
   157 |     stateOfFormation: stateMatch ? stateMatch[1].trim() : undefined,
   158 |     ein: einMatch ? einMatch[1] : undefined,
   159 |     formationDate: dateMatch ? dateMatch[1].trim() : undefined,
   160 |   };
   161 | }
   162 | 
   163 | export function extractPeriodFromFilename(filename: string): string | undefined {
   164 |   // Match patterns like: Dec2023, Dec_2023, December 2024, 01-2024, 2024-01
   165 |   // Note: no \b anchors because underscores are word characters so they break boundaries
   166 |   const patterns = [
   167 |     /(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)_?(\d{4})/i,
   168 |     /(january|february|march|april|may|june|july|august|september|october|november|december)[_\s-]?(\d{4})/i,
   169 |     /(\d{1,2})[_-](\d{4})/,
   170 |     /(\d{4})[_-](\d{1,2})/,
   171 |   ];
   172 | 
   173 |   for (const pat of patterns) {
   174 |     const match = filename.match(pat);
   175 |     if (match && match[1] && match[2]) {
   176 |       return `${match[1]} ${match[2]}`;
   177 |     }
   178 |   }
   179 |   return undefined;
   180 | }
   181 | 
   182 | export function extractPeriodFromText(text: string): string | undefined {
   183 |   const match =
   184 |     text.match(/statement period[:\s]+([^\n]{5,30})/i) ??
   185 |     text.match(/for the month of[:\s]+([^\n]{3,20})/i);
   186 |   if (match) return match[1].trim();
   187 |   return undefined;
   188 | }
   189 | 
   190 | function parseTransactions(text: string): Transaction[] {
   191 |   const transactions: Transaction[] = [];
   192 |   const lines = text.split("\n");
   193 | 
   194 |   // Simple CSV-like detection: date, description, amount
   195 |   const txPattern = /(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}(?:\/\d{2,4})?)\s+(.+?)\s+([+-]?\$?[\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/;
   196 | 
   197 |   for (const line of lines) {
   198 |     const match = line.match(txPattern);
   199 |     if (match) {
   200 |       const amount = parseAmount(match[3].replace(/\$/, ""));
   201 |       transactions.push({
   202 |         date: match[1],
   203 |         description: match[2].trim(),
   204 |         amount: Math.abs(amount),
   205 |         type: amount >= 0 ? "credit" : "debit",
   206 |       });
   207 |     }
   208 |   }
   209 | 
   210 |   return transactions.slice(0, 500); // cap at 500
   211 | }
   212 | 
   213 | function parseAmount(str: string): number {
   214 |   return parseFloat(str.replace(/,/g, "")) || 0;
   215 | }

     1 | import { classify, classifyFromContent, DocumentType } from "./classifier";
     2 | import { extract, ExtractedData } from "./extractor";
     3 | import { analyzeBankStatement } from "./bank-analyzer";
     4 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     5 | 
     6 | export interface PipelineInput {
     7 |   documentId: string;
     8 |   clientId: string;
     9 |   filename: string;
    10 |   mimeType: string;
    11 |   buffer: Buffer;
    12 |   documentType?: string;
    13 | }
    14 | 
    15 | export interface PipelineResult {
    16 |   documentType: DocumentType;
    17 |   confidence: number;
    18 |   extractedData: ExtractedData;
    19 |   period?: string;
    20 | }
    21 | 
    22 | export async function runIngestionPipeline(input: PipelineInput): Promise<PipelineResult> {
    23 |   const { documentId, filename, mimeType, buffer, documentType: providedType } = input;
    24 | 
    25 |   // Step 1: Classify
    26 |   let classification = classify(filename, mimeType);
    27 | 
    28 |   // Try to get text content for better classification (for text-based files)
    29 |   let textContent = "";
    30 |   if (mimeType === "text/csv" || mimeType === "text/plain") {
    31 |     textContent = buffer.toString("utf-8");
    32 |     const contentClassification = classifyFromContent(filename, textContent, mimeType);
    33 |     if (contentClassification.confidence > classification.confidence) {
    34 |       classification = contentClassification;
    35 |     }
    36 |   }
    37 | 
    38 |   const documentType = (providedType as DocumentType) ?? classification.documentType;
    39 |   const confidence = classification.confidence;
    40 | 
    41 |   // Step 2: Extract data
    42 |   const extractedData = extract(documentType, filename, textContent);
    43 | 
    44 |   // Step 3: If bank statement, run bank analysis and save
    45 |   if (documentType === "BANK_STATEMENT") {
    46 |     const analysis = analyzeBankStatement(extractedData, filename);
    47 | 
    48 |     await prisma.bankStatementAnalysis.upsert({
    49 |       where: { documentId },
    50 |       update: {
    51 |         period: analysis.period,
    52 |         totalDeposits: analysis.totalDeposits,
    53 |         totalWithdrawals: analysis.totalWithdrawals,
    54 |         avgMonthlyDeposits: analysis.avgMonthlyDeposits,
    55 |         avgMonthlyWithdrawals: analysis.avgMonthlyWithdrawals,
    56 |         netCashflow: analysis.netCashflow,
    57 |         overdraftCount: analysis.overdraftCount,
    58 |         nsfCount: analysis.nsfCount,
    59 |         recurringTransactions: JSON.stringify(analysis.recurringTransactions),
    60 |         lenderReadySummary: JSON.stringify(analysis.lenderReadySummary),
    61 |       },
    62 |       create: {
    63 |         documentId,
    64 |         period: analysis.period,
    65 |         totalDeposits: analysis.totalDeposits,
    66 |         totalWithdrawals: analysis.totalWithdrawals,
    67 |         avgMonthlyDeposits: analysis.avgMonthlyDeposits,
    68 |         avgMonthlyWithdrawals: analysis.avgMonthlyWithdrawals,
    69 |         netCashflow: analysis.netCashflow,
    70 |         overdraftCount: analysis.overdraftCount,
    71 |         nsfCount: analysis.nsfCount,
    72 |         recurringTransactions: JSON.stringify(analysis.recurringTransactions),
    73 |         lenderReadySummary: JSON.stringify(analysis.lenderReadySummary),
    74 |       },
    75 |     });
    76 | 
    77 |     extractedData.period = analysis.period;
    78 |   }
    79 | 
    80 |   return {
    81 |     documentType,
    82 |     confidence,
    83 |     extractedData,
    84 |     period: extractedData.period,
    85 |   };
    86 | }

     4 | function getOAuthClient(): OAuth2Client {

     6 |     process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
     7 |     process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET

    14 | export async function createClientFolder(
    15 |   businessName: string,
    16 |   parentFolderId?: string
    17 | ): Promise<string> {
    18 |   const auth = getOAuthClient();
    19 |   const drive = google.drive({ version: "v3", auth });
    20 | 
    21 |   const folderName = `BBA - ${businessName} - ${new Date().getFullYear()}`;
    22 |   const parent = parentFolderId ?? process.env.GOOGLE_DRIVE_FOLDER_ID;
    23 | 
    24 |   const res = await drive.files.create({
    25 |     requestBody: {
    26 |       name: folderName,
    27 |       mimeType: "application/vnd.google-apps.folder",
    28 |       parents: parent ? [parent] : undefined,
    29 |     },
    30 |     fields: "id",
    31 |   });
    32 | 
    33 |   if (!res.data.id) {
    34 |     throw new Error("Failed to create Google Drive folder");
    35 |   }
    36 | 
    37 |   return res.data.id;
    38 | }
    39 | 
    40 | export async function uploadFileToDrive(
    41 |   folderId: string,
    42 |   filename: string,
    43 |   mimeType: string,
    44 |   buffer: Buffer
    45 | ): Promise<string> {
    46 |   const auth = getOAuthClient();
    47 |   const drive = google.drive({ version: "v3", auth });
    48 | 
    49 |   const { Readable } = await import("stream");
    50 |   const stream = Readable.from(buffer);
    51 | 
    52 |   const res = await drive.files.create({
    53 |     requestBody: {
    54 |       name: filename,
    55 |       parents: [folderId],
    56 |     },
    57 |     media: {
    58 |       mimeType,
    59 |       body: stream,
    60 |     },
    61 |     fields: "id, webViewLink",
    62 |   });
    63 | 
    64 |   return res.data.webViewLink ?? res.data.id ?? "";
    65 | }
    66 |

    69 |   subfolderName: string
    70 | ): Promise<string> {
    71 |   const auth = getOAuthClient();
    72 |   const drive = google.drive({ version: "v3", auth });
    73 | 
    74 |   // Check if subfolder already exists
    75 |   const existing = await drive.files.list({
    76 |     q: `name='${subfolderName}' and '${parentFolderId}' in parents and mimeType='application/vnd.google-apps.folder' and trashed=false`,
    77 |     fields: "files(id, name)",
    78 |   });
    79 | 
    80 |   if (existing.data.files && existing.data.files.length > 0) {
    81 |     return existing.data.files[0].id!;
    82 |   }
    83 | 
    84 |   // Create it
    85 |   const res = await drive.files.create({
    86 |     requestBody: {
    87 |       name: subfolderName,
    88 |       mimeType: "application/vnd.google-apps.folder",
    89 |       parents: [parentFolderId],
    90 |     },
    91 |     fields: "id",
    92 |   });
    93 | 
    94 |   return res.data.id!;
    95 | }
```

     1 | import { Client as NotionClient } from "@notionhq/client";
     2 | 
     3 | let notionClient: NotionClient | null = null;
     4 | 
     5 | function getNotionClient(): NotionClient {
     6 |   if (!notionClient) {
     7 |     if (!process.env.NOTION_TOKEN) {
     8 |       throw new Error("NOTION_TOKEN environment variable is not set");
     9 |     }
    10 |     notionClient = new NotionClient({ auth: process.env.NOTION_TOKEN });
    11 |   }
    12 |   return notionClient;
    13 | }
    14 | 
    15 | export interface NotionClientRecord {
    16 |   businessName: string;
    17 |   ownerName: string;
    18 |   ownerEmail: string;
    19 |   stage: string;
    20 |   programTrack?: string;
    21 |   readinessScore: number;
    22 | }
    23 | 
    24 | export async function createNotionClientRecord(
    25 |   databaseId: string,
    26 |   record: NotionClientRecord
    27 | ): Promise<string> {
    28 |   const notion = getNotionClient();
    29 | 
    30 |   const response = await notion.pages.create({
    31 |     parent: { database_id: databaseId },
    32 |     properties: {
    33 |       Name: {
    34 |         title: [{ text: { content: record.businessName } }],
    35 |       },
    36 |       "Owner Name": {
    37 |         rich_text: [{ text: { content: record.ownerName } }],
    38 |       },
    39 |       "Owner Email": {
    40 |         email: record.ownerEmail,
    41 |       },
    42 |       Stage: {
    43 |         select: { name: record.stage },
    44 |       },
    45 |       "Program Track": {
    46 |         rich_text: record.programTrack
    47 |           ? [{ text: { content: record.programTrack } }]
    48 |           : [],
    49 |       },
    50 |       "Readiness Score": {
    51 |         number: record.readinessScore,
    52 |       },
    53 |     },
    54 |   });
    55 | 
    56 |   return response.id;
    57 | }
    58 | 
    59 | export async function updateNotionClientStage(
    60 |   pageId: string,
    61 |   stage: string
    62 | ): Promise<void> {
    63 |   const notion = getNotionClient();
    64 | 
    65 |   await notion.pages.update({
    66 |     page_id: pageId,
    67 |     properties: {
    68 |       Stage: {
    69 |         select: { name: stage },
    70 |       },
    71 |     },
    72 |   });
    73 | }
    74 | 
    75 | export async function queryNotionDatabase(
    76 |   databaseId: string,
    77 |   filter?: Parameters<NotionClient["databases"]["query"]>[0]["filter"]
    78 | ) {
    79 |   const notion = getNotionClient();
    80 | 
    81 |   const response = await notion.databases.query({
    82 |     database_id: databaseId,
    83 |     filter,
    84 |   });
    85 | 
    86 |   return response.results;
    87 | }

     1 | import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
     2 | import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
     3 | import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
     4 | 
     5 | export async function getPortalClient() {
     6 |   const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
     7 |   if (!session) {
     8 |     return { session: null, client: null };
     9 |   }
    10 | 
    11 |   const client = await prisma.client.findUnique({
    12 |     where: { userId: session.user.id },
    13 |     select: {
    14 |       id: true,
    15 |       businessName: true,
    16 |       stage: true,
    17 |     },
    18 |   });
    19 | 
    20 |   return { session, client };
    21 | }
```

    17 |   ownerPhone: z.string().min(10, "Phone number must be at least 10 digits"),

    24 |         email: z.string().email(),

    27 |     )
    28 |     .optional(),
    29 | });
    30 |

    34 |   monthlyRevenue: z.coerce.number().optional(),
    35 |   businessBankAccount: z.enum(["yes", "no"]),

    40 |

    45 |     .array(
    46 |       z.enum([

    48 |         "section-179",

    60 |   previousFundingAttempts: z.enum(["never", "rejected", "approved-insufficient", "other"]),
    61 |   previousFundingDetails: z.string().optional(),

    63 |

    72 |       "k1",
    73 |       "formation-docs",
    74 |       "invoices",
    75 |       "contracts",
    76 |       "id",
    77 |       "none",
    78 |     ])
    79 |   ),
    80 | });
    81 | 
    82 | // Full intake form
    83 | export const intakeFormSchema = z.object({
    84 |   ...step1Schema.shape,

    90 |

    97 | 
    98 | export type IntakeFormData = z.infer<typeof intakeFormSchema>;
    99 | export type IntakeSubmissionData = z.infer<typeof intakeSubmissionSchema>;

   102 | export type Step3Data = z.infer<typeof step3Schema>;

     9 |   provider = "sqlite"
    10 |   url      = env("DATABASE_URL")

    12 | 
    13 | // Role values: SUPER_ADMIN | PROGRAM_MANAGER | COMPLIANCE | FINANCE | CLIENT_SUCCESS | CREDIT | CLIENT
    14 | // ClientStage values: LEAD | INTAKE | STRATEGY | FOUNDATION | ACTIVE | FUNDED | CLOSED
    15 | // DocumentType values: BANK_STATEMENT | TAX_RETURN | W2 | FORM_1099 | K1 | FORMATION_DOC | INVOICE | CONTRACT | ID_DOCUMENT | OTHER
    16 | // DocumentStatus values: PENDING | PROCESSING | PROCESSED | FAILED | REJECTED

    18 | // TaskPriority values: LOW | MEDIUM | HIGH | URGENT

    20 | // NotificationType values: INFO | WARNING | SUCCESS | ERROR
    21 | // QuestionType values: TEXT | TEXTAREA | SELECT | MULTISELECT | RADIO | CHECKBOX | DATE | NUMBER | FILE
    22 | 
    23 | model User {
    24 |   id             String    @id @default(cuid())
    25 |   name           String?
    26 |   email          String    @unique
    27 |   emailVerified  DateTime?
    28 |   image          String?
    29 |   role           String    @default("CLIENT")
    30 |   hashedPassword String?
    31 |   createdAt      DateTime  @default(now())
    32 |   updatedAt      DateTime  @updatedAt
    33 | 
    34 |   accounts      Account[]

    36 |   client        Client?
    37 |   assignedTasks Task[]        @relation("AssignedTasks")
    38 |   notes         Note[]
    39 |   auditLogs     AuditLog[]
    40 |   notifications Notification[]
    41 | }
    42 | 
    43 | model Account {
    44 |   id                String  @id @default(cuid())
    45 |   userId            String
    46 |   type              String
    47 |   provider          String
    48 |   providerAccountId String
    49 |   refresh_token     String?

    51 |   expires_at        Int?
